HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"101:5:2","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_0161b54664a0cd4c383d","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"imperfect copular becoming into a predicate state, with feminine singular agreement to the non-rational broken plural subject\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:تَكُونُ}} ({{tr:takūnu}}) is the clause's becoming verb, not an existential statement that mountains simply occur. It links {{ar:ٱلْجِبَالُ}} ({{tr:al-jibālu}}) to the predicate {{ar:كَٱلْعِهْنِ ٱلْمَنفُوشِ}} ({{tr:ka-l-ʿihni al-manfūsh}}), so the most stable visible bodies are assigned a coming state of loosened fiber. Because the form is copular rather than causative, the mountains are grammatically subject without becoming agents; they undergo the state. Its feminine singular prefix marks the non-rational broken plural subject and makes the shift from the people clause in 101:4 audible inside the grammar. The repeated becoming frame across 101:4 and {{ar:تَكُونُ}} ({{tr:takūnu}}) here creates a shared mechanism of dissolution, while the ordinary being verb becomes marked by rare wool and carding partners; 70:9 repeats the mountain-wool formula without the final processed-wool adjective, so this ayah gives the same frame a more worked-apart ending.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ك و ن}} ({{tr:k-w-n}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"broad being, happening, existing, and becoming range; the local copular frame selects state-assignment rather than independent existence or causative making\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:تَكُونُ}} ({{tr:takūnu}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"101:5:5","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_0293f87a72fae26d79cc","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"definite passive participle modifying the wool: carded, teased apart, scattered, and presented as a resultant processed state\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:ٱلْمَنفُوشِ}} ({{tr:al-manfūsh}}) is the ayah's closing specification: the image does not stop at wool, but at wool already carded, teased apart, and dispersed. Its passive participle form foregrounds the affected material and leaves the processor unnamed, so the mountains are heard as a patient of a completed state rather than as actors. Concord with {{ar:ٱلْعِهْنِ}} ({{tr:al-ʿihn}}) makes this processed condition inseparable from the comparison itself. The root also appears in 21:78 for active animal scattering, which helps the reader feel both deliberate carding and uncontrolled dispersal while the local form narrows the range to passive processed material. The final-word position, rarity of the form, and soft nasal-to-fricative sound make this the audible landing point of the two-ayah dissolution; it also echoes the passive dispersal of the people in 101:4 before the sorting grammar opens in 101:6.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ن ف ش}} ({{tr:n-f-sh}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"carding, teasing apart, spreading, and scattering range, including an active animal-scattering use elsewhere; the local passive participle selects processed wool-like dispersal\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:ٱلْمَنفُوشِ}} textile.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QS-5fd72c58\",\"QS-b873e889\",\"QY-28d6ad25\"],\"status\":\"narrowed\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"101:5:3:nonrational-agreement-shift","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_2ad3d61c825bbc6babc6","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"broken The definite broken plural makes the subject generic and comprehensive, not one local mountain, and its nominative role keeps the mountains distinct from the wool image that describes them. As a non-rational broken plural it also controls feminine singular agreement, so the boundary movement from people in 101:4 to mountains here is marked in grammar as well as scale. The root's formed-mass pressure matters because the ayah reverses what mountains normally signify: permanence, elevation, and made solidity are placed under a predicate of worked fiber. Stability passages such as 78:7 and 31:10 sharpen that reversal, while the repeated mountain-wool formula in 70:9 shows that this is a recognized eschatological pairing. The attached comparison particle makes the mountains like wool rather than simply identifying them as wool, and the whole phrase functions as the predicate of {{ar:تَكُونُ}} ({{tr:takūnu}}). Its fused surface makes the comparison inseparable from the wool noun at the very point where the clause pivots from mountain subject to material image. The rare wool word carries softness, color, and separable-fiber pressure, so geological mass is imagined under a material that can be worked apart.
